KI UM JER KSIAR 

KA TEMPLE JONG KA JINGSTAD ka plie rynghang 
bad miet bad sngi ia la ki Jingkhang bad ki Khyrdop sha 
kito baroh kiba mon ban rung shapoh jang Ka. Hapdeng 
jong Ka, ka ieng ka Duwan Ksiar ba la shat pyniphieng da 
ki Mawlynnai bad ki Mawkordor ba la lum na ki jingtrei 
minor U Khun Bynriew shityllup ka Pyrthei. Ki ’Rang 
bad ki ’Thei Kynsai kiba la ioh ban rung shapoh jong Ka, 
ki rah ha ki kti jong ki ia ki Song ba la buh ha ki Pliang 
Ksiar, kum ki Jingkftia Ainguh Thang ba iwbih ban 
pyndonburom ia la U Trai Nongthaw Nongbuh Nongseng 
bad Nongsei la ka Long Rynieng, bad U Blei Nongpyniaid 
ka Pyrthei Mariang. 

KA TEMPLE jong ka JINGSTAD ka ieng kynshreng 
ha kliar u Lumbah, bad ki Jaid Bynriew baroh ka Pyrthei 
ki ialeh ban kiew sha Ka ban buh la ki Pansngiat Ksiar 
ka Jingtrei minot ha kjat ka Duwan jong Ka. U Khun 
Khasi ruh naduh ba la mih ka Thoh ka Tar, u la ioh bynta 
lem ha kaba noh la ki Pansngiat Ksiar ha khmat jong Ka 

Te, lada lum ia ki IJMJER KSIAR na ki um Tynriew, 
ne, na ki Sla Wang Sla Syntiew, kam pher eiei. Ynda la

pynlang ia ki ha ki Tyndong bad ki Klong, ki pynshlei miar 
ia ki Phiang bad ki Raklang. Lada la lum ia ki jar-jar 
hapdeng ki Ummat ka Synnia, ne, ha ki Thwei ba kynjah 
ka ’Weibriew, kam pher eiei. Ki kjat Sngi ba phymai jong 
ka step ki pyntyngshain ia ki kum ki Rubi bad ki Larmorti 
ha ki rong ki rup ba itynnad, bad ki niad noh phar ia ki 
Jingjaw Ummat jong ka Synnia. * — - 

Te, jar-jar ha ka miet ba synnia la sarfl fhir/ # ki 
UMJER KSIAR, bad tang ki Khlur, na tiynO&t, Jca sahit 
bneng ki iohi kumno ba ki lang halor ki/Tflg^Fyhriew bad 
ki Ing sop Tin bad ba ki Thaw ia ki ThwaTka Jingkyrmen 
ban ai jhgtngen ia U Khun Bynriew ibp ialeh ban kiew 
sha Sympcr jong katci ka TEMPLE. * v 

Te, ka Ri Shyiap Arabia bad ki J^yntha ka Ri India 
kin phuh bad kin tngen, namar ki Umjer Kkiar ba la lum ha 
ki Synnia jong ka Miet ; bad ka Ri KhaSi jonfe ki Lum, ki 
Wah bad ki Them ba itynnad kin kren lirjjflpY ha ka sur , 
bad ka dur ba iadei bad ka jinglong jong Ka hi. Ki.Umjer 
Ksiar ki synreit ban pynjem ia ki lynti ba kyjlain jong u

sljang. Te, la ki long tang kiei kiei kiba rit bad ba jah noh 
tang shu icng ka Sngi, ki don la ka dor bym lah sep. 

Ka Sngi ka ieit ia ki KJiar Lum Makashang bad ka doh 
ia ka lor ba la pynlang da ki Jynhaw Um bad ki Umjer Ksiar. 
Shisha, ka Pyrthei hi ka ieng bad ka peit lyngngoh ia ki jing-

kylla ba phylla ki Umjer sha ki Lyoh bad ka lor ba ithiang. 

Te, la lum ia kine ki Jingthoh ba la jer, “ICi Umjer 
Ksiar” na kylleng — nabar bad napoh ka Ri : ngi kyrmen 
ba kin pynlang sha ki Khuift ka Thoh ka Tar (. Khasi Litera- 
ture) la shai la shisngi. 

Ngi la shim ruh katto katne ki symboh na ki Nongthoh «ba 
kynsai ka Ri— Mr. MONDON BAREH, Mr. SOSO THAM 
Mr. ONDROMONEY, bad kiwei kiwei de kiba nga la jer ha 
ka jingkdew. Ngi shem ba ka long ka burom ia ka Ri 
lada lah ban ioh ia ki Jingthoh ki ’Riew Khraw jong ngi 
ban lum shi jaka. Kane kan long ka jingmait lynti kaba 
thymmai, bad lehse ngin dang ioh lad ban lum kum kata 
ka Kot ha kine ki khyndiat snem, bad ban buh ruh bad ki 
dur jong ki de. 

Kane ka long ka KHASI COLLECTION kaba Thymmai. 
bad ka don ruh rukom rukom ki style ha ka. Ki Nongpule 
— kiba shong skul bad kiba la tjvi kan Kei duk, ha 
Kyndong bad ha So;, ngi ngeit kin myntoi lada kin ihied 
bad pule ia ka. Khamtan kilci ki Khvnnah Skul k' ban 
dang pule pat bun na ki ha ki Skul Bah (Colleges) lasjiai 
lashisngi. Hangne ki don — Greek Mythology, Biography, 
Scientific Subjects, Essays (Narrative, Descriptive & Sub- 
jective), bad Short Stories. 

Ka jingangnud jong ngi ka dei ban long lano ba ka 
University kan ibit ban pynrung ia ka Ktien jong ngi 
kum ka Major Vernacular. Kane ka dei ban ktah jur 
ia kiRangbah ka Ri bad ki K nie ki Kpa ki Khynnah 
Skul ; lymda kumta, ki Khun jong ngi kin shitom ha ki 
Competitive Examination. 

Khadduh khadwai, ngi iehnoh Jia phi ki Nongpule ban

tih, wad bad bishar hok ia ki jingmyntoi ba phin ioh na kine 
ki Umjer Ksiar. 

Jaiaw Hostel, Shillong, "]

1. KA KH1M THERMOPYLAE 
(U Lkonidas Bad Ki Khi awaii Jong L))

khraw uba kyrteng u Xerxes bad u la synshar halor ka 
ri Persia baroh kawei. La u long u Syiem jong ka 
ri Persia bad la u la synshar Iajan halor ki jaid bynriew

shym la dei u doctor. U brtow uba lap ia kaae ka jjngitad.

20. KA PANDORA

22. U ORPHEUS BAD KA DUITARA JONG U 

Ka jingruwai ka don ka bor kaba phylla haduh kalno 
halor ki samla bad ki tymmen kumjuh. Ka lah ban 
pyndap ia ki dohnud jong ngi da ki jingpyrkhat kiba don 
burom ; ka lah bah pynkmen ne pynsngewsih ia ngi. 
Lyngba bun ki spah snem ba la iaid lait, ka jingruwai ka la 
wallam ka jingkmen kaba khraw ha ka pyrthei. Ha ki 
khanatang jong ki Greek bad ki Roman hyndai, ngi pule 
ba don katto katne ngut na ki nongthaw jingruwai ba thiang 
ba ki la don ka bor kaba kham khraw shibun halor ki 
briew ban ia ki syiem, shynrang bad kynthei, kiba khraw 
bor tarn. 

Ha ki por ba dang lung ka pyrthei la don uwei u 
nongruwai bad nongtem uba phylla tarn ha ri Greece, uba 
kyrteng U Orpheus. La ju fiiew ia u ba u long u myllung 
bad u rangphawar ba stad tarn jong ki juk baroh. Ym 
pat ju mm u nongput nongtem uba kat ma u mynno 
mynno ruh. Haba u ruwai bad tern la ka duitara ka pyrthei 
bad mariang baroh ka iap ngiah : ka eriong bad u 
kyllang ki jem jai haba u tem : ki dew kiba shad kynting 
jong ka duriaw ki thiah suk lyngngai haba u ruwai bad la ka 
duitara. Ki dieng ki nguh la ki kliar bad ki padoi sngew- 
tynnadkumba pynshad ka erbatemon : ki maw ramsong 
bad ki lum rit ki thap kti bad ki shad kmen, Wat Id new 
sniew hi ki klet ia la ki lynti bad ki jingpyrkhat bymman 
haba ki bud ia u haba u ruwai ne u tem, bad ki sngew

kumba la rong noh ia ki sha kawei pat ka pyrthei bad

u syiem jong ka ri ka jingiap. Hangta u la shem ia u Syiem

35. KA REP PHAN HA RI KHAS1. 

Ka ri Khasi ka dei ka ri rep. Lait noh na ki katto 
katne kiba sbong ha sor Shillong, u paid ka ri baroh u 
im da ka rep. Ki jingrep ba kongsan ki long u kba, u 
phan, u soh, u kwai u tympew. 

U phan u long uwei rn ki jingthung ba pawkhmat bad 
ba iadei bha ha ka ’dew ri Khasi. La khot ia u, u phan 
myngngor, namar la ju ngeit ba u la don ha ri India naduh 
ka jingsynshar ki Patsha Mughol, kiba la jop ia ka, jin 
haduh ki lam Assam. Dei u David Scott, uba ki Khasi ki 
khot u Iskat Sahep uba la wallam nyngkong eh ia une 
u phan sha ri Khasi ha ka snent 182d. Mynta u la kylla 
tong ka spah bah ka ri jong ngi. U Sahep David Scott 
u la bret shisha da ka tyngka pliah ia ki lu;n ki wah mynba

ba im lymbiang jong ngi. U la tei in lade u mot ba im 
uba kham jrong bun shah ia u mot ha Sohra. Ki briew 
kin iai kynmaw ia n katba ki dang rep phan.

41. U PATSHA KA LONGKUR 

(U SYNTIEW JUBAN LAK) 

Ka jingim kaba bha kam ju bishni iano iano ruh ; ka ieit 
bad ka iaishah slem bad ki para briew baroh ha sla kane ka 
pyrthei : kam ju fiiewbein ne peit shiliang khmat ia u rit,

ka leh hok salonsar. Ka long kaba iaineh bad ka bym ju 
kylla. U briow uba don ka jingim ba bha bad ka jingieit

nusib jong u, u maw kordor, u lar u morti, ne, u moifi u

ia beh, bad ka long ruh ka tynrai ka jingim ka ri Khasi bad 
ri Pnar. 

Naduh kulong kumah, u khun Khasi Pnar, shuwa ba 
un kren ia kano kano ka ktien, u don ka jingtieng burom ia 
la U Trai Nongthaw, Nongbuh Nongsaifi Mynsiem. U khan 
ha la ka dohnud ia ka dei bad ka lait, nangta u sa khlei la 
ka jutang (word ofhonour ), bad, dei na kata ka daw, ba u

ia ka siar ka lait kylla ha la ka long bnew. Kumta u da 
pynshong ia la ki ktien, ha kata ka nongrim jong ka 
jingbha, kaba don kite! ki mawkordor, kiba kham soh bad 
kham rem ban ia ka ksiar bad ka rupa. Dei ia kine ki 
ktien kein ba ki jer “Ki Syntiew Jaban Lak,” ha ri Khasi 
Pnar, kiba mih na u briew uba bha. Kum ia uta u briew, 
uba don ka pansngiat jong ka jingshai kaba khuid suba 
phar, la ju khot ruh ia u, “U Patsha ka Longkur.” U bet 
1 a ka jingbha. bad um ju kren klop, ne kren tab. U sliang 
naduh la ka mynsiem ban im ka Bhalang. Um kwah ban

ia bun kiten ia ka ban ia pynrem pyndor, hynrei u kren tang

44 KI SNGI MUHARRAM HA KIRMANSHAH 

Kiba khambun na ki nongshong shnong ha Kirmanshah 
ki ioog ki Mohammedan, bad la jan baroh ki nong-Petrsia 
ki long na kane ka jingngeit. Ki Mohammedan ki don 
ar jaid, ki Shiahs bad ki Sunnis, bad ki Persian ki dei na 
ka jaid Shiahs. Ki Shiahs ki pher na ki Sunnis tang ha 
kawei ka jingiapher jingmut. 

Ki ngeit ba ki ar ngut ki khun jong u Ali, u pyrsakurim 
jong u Prophet Mohamed. ki long kiba dei hok ban ioh 
long Caliph ne Prophet. Dei namar kane ka jingiapher 
jingmut ba ki la ialeh ka thma kaba jur para ki hi ha 
Karbala.

U Ali, uba long u Caliph uba saw, u la long u pyrsa 
kurim jong u Prophet Mohamed. Une u la don ar ngut ki 
khun shynrang, u Husan bad u Husen. Hynrei ha ka por 
ba u dang im hi u Ali, la mih sa uwei pat u briew uba 
kyrteng u Muawiah u ruh u la pynbna sha ka pyrthei ba 
u long ma u uba dei hok ban long u Caliph, ym u Ali. Ha 
kata ka por u la don tang khyndiat ki nongbud, kiba bun 
hi ki dang peit sha u Ali kum u Caliph uba dei hok shisha. 
Hynrei ynda u la iap u Ali, u Husan u khun nyngkong 
jong u, namar ba u long u briew u bym kwah ia ka kajia 
majia bad uba kwah tang kurano ban nym mih ka jingsniew, 
u la iehnch ia ka jinglong Caliph bad u la aiti noh 
ha u Muawiah. U khun jong une u Muawiah uba kyrteng

ban nym don jingeh eiei shuh ia u hadien habud. Ha 
kaba khadduh uba pli u Hasan u la iap da kaba ai bih ki 
briew bad ia kane la leh da ka jingthaw buit jong u Aziz. 

U Husen u la long u khun uba ar jong u Ali bad u 
la long ruh kum u kynja Syietn ha Medina. Ia une ki nong 
Kufa ki la kular ban ia kyrshah ban pynieng biang ia ka 
hok ha ka ban pynioh pat ha u ia ka jinglong Caliph na ka 
Ing jong u Muawiah ; hynrei kine da ka jingsuh buit jong 
u Aziz, ki la kylla bad Ki la shet kylla ia u. Urn banse 
ban iateh ia ki da khyndiat ngut ki briew kiba wan bud 
ia u. Ynda tun lah kyrshan shuh, u la phet iap, bad ki 
briew u Aziz ki la beh haduh ba kin da ioh pyniap ia u 
ha ki pyntha jong ka Karbala, ka shnong kaba hajan
